Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in heat theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 605 and the invariant I(A,B) = 326 mod 4.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to heat structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 605/326 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
